L1       classdef REPLANNING_SYSTEM_R23_COMPILED < REPLANNING_SYSTEM_R21_BALANCED
L2       % AF7R58ZZY-R23-R2 | THEORY-PRESERVING COMPILED CORRECTION AUTHORITY
L5       % Mathematical/certificate authority remains the exact R21/R19 canonical model:
L9       %   - continuous-time geometry certificate and sampled_acceptance=false
L12      %   - de Casteljau arithmetic and canonical correction-group addition order
L13      %   - speed/sensor/runtime packet certificates
L14      %   - trigger+2*dt staged activation and fail-closed deadline behavior
L15      %   - canonical eval_fn / flat_eval_fn retained as mathematical authority
L17      % Runtime-only changes:
L18      %   1) precompute derivative control points once after Stage2 certificate closure;
L19      %   2) current repaired xd uses exact nominal-table authority + compiled correction;
L20      %   3) rolling repaired packet uses exact nominal position/acceleration tables +
L21      %      compiled r={0,2} correction evaluator;
L22      %   4) Stage3 uses the same compiled evaluator for the non-authority cache;
L23      %   5) Stage3 freshness is checked once in advance_staged_replan_pipeline and
L25      %   6) Bfull materialization and post-assignment duplicate hot-guard are removed;
L29              r23_build_id = 'AF7R58ZZY_R23_COMPILED_V1_R3';
L31              r23_last_stage3_cache_ms = NaN;
L32              r23_last_stage3_target_guard_ms = NaN;
L33              r23_last_stage3_assignment_ms = NaN;
L34              r23_last_stage3_total_ms = NaN;
L35              r23_last_packet_ms = NaN;
L37              r23_compiled_current_count = 0;
L38              r23_compiled_packet_count = 0;
L39              r23_stage3_token_valid = false;
L40              r23_stage3_token_time = NaN;
L41              r23_stage3_token_source_version = NaN;
L42              r23_stage3_token_target_version = NaN;

L1       classdef REPLANNING_SYSTEM_R21_BALANCED < REPLANNING_SYSTEM_R19_FAST
L2       % AF7R58ZZY-R21 | THEORY-PRESERVING STAGE2/STAGE3 LOAD BALANCE
L5       % It changes only WHEN the non-authority post-commit reference cache is built:
L6       %   Stage2: close all continuous certificates, resource/batch model, runtime
L7       %           sweep/obstacle/sensor packet, evaluator handles, and immutable
L9       %   Stage3: after the same scheduled activation edge is reached, build the
L10      %           first repaired {pL,q} cache for t_commit+dt, then call the exact
L13      % The cache is not used to accept the trajectory or to close continuous-time
L14      % geometry/C7/speed safety. Any cache/materialization/deadline failure leaves
L15      % the current authority unchanged. Activation remains trigger+2*dt.

L781                 % 将来 REPAIRED 軌道 commit 用の共通インターフェース。

L795                     obj.obstacle_packet= ...

L800                     obj.build_sensor_horizon_certificate();

L5228            function [tf,reason] = can_reuse_reference_only_prepared_packet_cache_exact(obj,C,newSC)
L5229                % AF7R58ZL exact NON-AUTHORITY prepared-cache retarget gate.
L5231                % A cached entry is NOT accepted as a new safety certificate.
L5232                % It is accepted only as an exact static-geometry base from which
L5233                % the current-speed sweep/packet/sensor tuple is recomputed.
L5235                % Therefore payload/UAV speed changes do not invalidate the cache:
L5236                % the current speed certificate is applied exactly by
L5237                % retarget_reference_only_prepared_packet_cache_exact().
L5240                % 1 policy | 2 cache/schema | 3 newSC | 4 cachedSC
L5241                % 5 cached payload speed invalid | 6 cached UAV speed invalid
L5244                % 11 cached sweep mismatch | 12 cached packet invalid
L5245                % 13 cached sensor mismatch | 14 cached dep invalid

L5258                        isfield(C,'packet') && isfield(C,'sensor') && ...

L5282                VQcached=double(oldSC.uav_speed_UB_m_s);
L5284                if ~(isfinite(VLcached)&&VLcached>=0), return; end
L5286                if ~(isfinite(VQcached)&&VQcached>=0), return; end
L5292                    'horizon','sensor_range','cable_length','static_obstacle_map'};

L5320                % cached speed. It is only a base; current-speed quantities are
L5323                pay=VLcached*dt/2;
L5324                uav=VQcached*dt/2;
L5325                cab=max(VLcached,VQcached)*dt/2;
L5333                        isfield(S,'sensor_one_period_m') && ...
L5334                            isequal(double(S.sensor_one_period_m),VQcached*dt))
L5339                P=C.packet;
L5340                M=numel(obj.static_obstacle_map);
L5360                horizonMargin=Rs-(rho+VQcached*H);
L5361                dDetLB=Rs-VQcached*dt;
